# Agents · P · part 18

993 entries, planner-csk to platform-producthunt. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **planner-csk**: Planning specialist. Use proactively before implementing a feature whose scope or acceptance criteria are unclear: task breakdown + acceptance criteria + dependency order, via the `spec-planning` skill. Plans; does not write code. ([byerlikaya/claude-starter-kit](https://github.com/byerlikaya/claude-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/claude-starter/agents/planner-csk.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **planner-cyd**: Forrás: Firmware/planner-cyd/README.md (main checkout), Hub/data/topology.json roadmap, 2026-10-31 ([webagoly-work/vrg-toolset](https://github.com/webagoly-work/vrg-toolset/blob/HEAD/docs/hub/agents/planner-cyd.md) · ★2)
- **planner-deep**: Planificador profundo (Opus, esfuerzo xhigh). SOLO para Tier 3 o riesgo alto, y SOLO cuando el usuario pida delegar la planificación o apruebe tu propuesta de delegarla (ADR-0030 - la delegación es opt-in) - migraciones, cambios irreversibles, contratos externos, seguridad, o cambios que cruzan varios módulos. Solo lectura - nunca edita. Para Tier 2 usa planner. ([elkisdm/devground](https://github.com/elkisdm/devground/blob/HEAD/packages/sdd/orchestration/agents/planner-deep.md) · ★9)
- **planner-deep**: Planning agent for decisions that are expensive to reverse: architecture, cross-cutting design, data modelling, authorization models, and tradeoffs where a wrong choice compounds through everything built on top. Read-only, and it decides nothing on the user's behalf. Use planner-fast for mechanical breakdowns. ([santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit](https://github.com/santiagoposadag/aprendiz-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/planner-deep.md) · ★3)
- **planner-deep**: 複数モジュール横断・アーキテクチャ判断・大規模リファクタの設計を行う。web/ と dbml-studio/ の共有境界、DB スキーマ変更、認証フロー変更など影響が広い計画専用。小規模は planner を使う。コードは書かない。 ([lv-kit/dbml-editor](https://github.com/lv-kit/dbml-editor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/planner-deep.md) · ★1)
